Implements: ISerializable

Constructors

Ring: new AccessViolationException(args) uses one constructor; the others are chosen by the parameter names: new AccessViolationException().@New([ :param = value ]) or dnNew("AccessViolationException",[ :param = value ]).

Members of the base class are inherited.
